# Corpus statistics — toolshed

Generated by `scripts/corpus_check.py --source toolshed` on 2026-05-26. Swept 8664 tools across 7638 repositories.

This file is regenerated by every full run of `corpus_check.py` for this source unless `--no-stats` is given; partial sweeps (`--limit` or `--repo`) do not regenerate it. Per-repo version labels make the snapshot reproducible.
